# Buildroot 2026.08 kconfig symbol list (vendored)
# Provenance: buildroot-2026.08 @ commit d5180309b1b66ef3b8eaccca70ad69be8e0729a1 (tag 2026.08, 2026-09-04)
# Regenerate (from a Buildroot 2026.08 checkout, as <BR2>):
#   cd <BR2> && find . -name Config.in* -print0 | xargs -0 grep -hE "^(config|menuconfig) [A-Za-z0-9_]+" \
#     | sed -E "s/^(config|menuconfig) +//" | sort -u
# One symbol per line; # lines are comments. This is the authoritative
# "does this symbol exist in pinned Buildroot 2026.08" set the linter
# verifier checks against.
#
# NOTE: menuconfig symbols MUST be included — they are settable in
# defconfigs too (e.g. BR2_TARGET_GRUB2 is a menuconfig). The original
# 9132-symbol snapshot captured only "^config" lines and silently
# dropped all 62 menuconfig symbols, which would have made the
# REQUIRED verifier reject a legitimate BR2_TARGET_GRUB2=y.
